# Current operational-savings automation handoff

This handoff captures PR #30 while the automation task continues and is intended to support a separate manual repair of all 54 Information Cards.
It does not modify PR #29, any Information Card, any production resource, or any deployment.

## Proof interpretation

Current proof levels below are the fail-closed levels from the generated proof ledger.
The ledger has no current content-bound run record, so implementation declarations are shown separately and must not be read as independently verified execution.

- Pending validation: the full real and portable suites, post-freeze container replay, proof attestation, typecheck, build, audits, and final generated-document validation.

- Keys and joins: Key each registration by category, context, source release, unit, and approved selector. Apply exact filters within one retained population and never join or pool unrelated registrations.
- Normalized targets: benchmark_populations, benchmark_values, calculation_runs, selected_values, selected_value_provenance.

- Parser paths: `scripts/research/operational-savings/adapters/context-benchmarks/inspect-schema.mjs`.
- Resolver paths: `scripts/research/operational-savings/adapters/context-benchmarks/run.mjs`.
- Tests: `scripts/research/operational-savings/tests/context-benchmarks-real.test.mjs` (resolves one exact dishwasher class and reaches annual racks); `scripts/research/operational-savings/tests/context-benchmarks-real.test.mjs` (publishes exact official dishwasher and cooktop benchmark cells); `scripts/research/operational-savings/tests/context-benchmarks-real.test.mjs` (fails changed benchmark cells and worksheet shapes); `scripts/research/operational-savings/tests/context-argonne-forklift-real.test.mjs` (maps both exact ITC-30 hourly intensity terms); `scripts/research/operational-savings/tests/context-argonne-forklift-real.test.mjs` (fails closed for every incompatible class or duty dimension); `scripts/research/operational-savings/tests/context-doe-walkin-real.test.mjs` (publishes both exact ITC-49 formula terms with canonical units and scope); `scripts/research/operational-savings/tests/context-doe-walkin-real.test.mjs` (fails closed outside the reviewed row and exact class dimensions); `scripts/research/operational-savings/tests/context-doe-walkin-real.test.mjs` (fails source-title, table-row, and fixture-value mutations); `scripts/research/operational-savings/tests/context-watersense-flush-real.test.mjs` (reaches the exact ITC-33 group annual flush term without fixture-count multiplication); `scripts/research/operational-savings/tests/context-watersense-flush-real.test.mjs` (fails closed for unproved visitors, subset allocation, and incompatible populations); `scripts/research/operational-savings/tests/context-benchmarks-real.test.mjs` (resolves an exact outdoor subsector and reaches ITC-02 existing_kW); `scripts/research/operational-savings/tests/context-benchmarks-real.test.mjs` (fails Table 4-29 header, application, and value mutations); `scripts/research/operational-savings/tests/context-fema-real.test.mjs` (maps the narrow FEMA calculation to the exact ITC-54 term); `scripts/research/operational-savings/tests/context-fema-real.test.mjs` (fails closed outside the diesel full-load boundary); `scripts/research/operational-savings/tests/context-fema-real.test.mjs` (fails native-formula and applicability mutations).

- Exact required inputs: Registered category and context, exact eligibility filters, pinned authoritative population, unit, minimum sample, and approved selection rule. [CALLER_OR_UPSTREAM_CATEGORY_PROCESS].
- Exact outputs: existing_kW (kW/fixture, PER_FIXTURE); annual_racks_per_unit (racks/year, PER_EQUIPMENT_UNIT); existing_fuel_per_hour (fuel-unit/hour, PER_HOUR); proposed_kWh_per_hour (kWh/hour, PER_HOUR); total_annual_flushes_group (flushes/year, PROJECT_TOTAL); benchmark_annual_test_fuel_per_unit (fuel-unit/year, PER_EQUIPMENT_UNIT); current_annual_refrigeration_kWh (kWh/year, PER_EQUIPMENT_UNIT); proposed_annual_refrigeration_kWh (kWh/year, PER_EQUIPMENT_UNIT); existing_resource_per_activity_r (resource/certified activity, PER_EVENT); proposed_resource_per_activity_r (resource/certified activity, PER_EVENT).
- Supported boundary: Only the reviewed category and context combinations represented by retained authoritative fixtures
- Unsupported boundary: A universal fallback, a cross-category median, or any context process without a pinned population and exact selection rule
- Current blocker: ITC-02 exterior lighting, the narrow ITC-30 paired forklift case, ITC-33 complete-group flush activity, the narrow ITC-49 walk-in component comparison, ITC-52 rack-dishwasher activity, and the narrow ITC-54 full-load diesel test-fuel process reach their category formulas. ITC-30 does not support a different forklift capacity, propulsion pair, or operating duty without another compatible authoritative population. ITC-33 visitor populations still require a male/female eligibility split, and subset fixture groups require an explicit allocation share or observed study. ITC-54 still has no source-derived annual test-hour schedule, standby-electricity benchmark, part-load curve, or non-diesel benchmark. ITC-49 supports only the ten reviewed DOE component rows, requires baseline and TSL 3 values from the same row, and cannot infer whole-box project energy. Every other context process still requires its own source-specific population and compatibility proof. No universal context fallback is permitted. The current content-bound proof execution record has not yet been created after source freeze.
- Remaining work: Freeze and commit all relevant implementation inputs. Create the current content-bound proof run and regenerate the ledger. Upload every required non-Git artifact to exact versioned research S3 objects and restore-verify it.
- Recommended reusable production strategy: Keep the shared selection contract but require every category benchmark to register its own source, filters, unit, population, minimum sample size, selected-value rule, and unsupported boundary.

## Reusable capability matrix

Requirement 1 means only a new category-local process is needed, requirement 2 means the Standard must be extended, requirement 3 means a genuinely new Standard is needed, and requirement 4 means this Standard has no currently defensible path for that capability.

|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| project-document-evidence-extraction | Project Document Evidence Extraction and Validation | Typed owned inputs, not a savings value. | ITC-01 through ITC-54 | Immutable document bytes and MIME type.<br>Declared document type and category schema.<br>Optional human confirmations. | Field claims with raw value, normalized value, unit, page or span or bounding box, document SHA-256, extraction method, and review status. | Field-native, PROJECT_OWNED_EVIDENCE |  | NEW_STANDARD_REQUIRED |
| exact-existing-equipment-record | Exact Existing Equipment Record Resolution | Existing native rating or intensity terms. | ITC-03<br>ITC-06<br>ITC-07<br>ITC-10<br>ITC-13<br>ITC-27<br>ITC-28<br>ITC-29<br>ITC-32<br>ITC-33<br>ITC-38<br>ITC-40<br>ITC-41<br>ITC-42<br>ITC-48<br>ITC-50<br>ITC-52<br>ITC-53 | Exact manufacturer, model, or basic-model identity.<br>Product class, status, and effective date. | One unambiguous compatible product record and its source-native ratings, or typed unavailable or ambiguous status. | Product-family-native, PER_EQUIPMENT_UNIT or PER_EVENT | STD-DOE-CCMS-RATINGS<br>STD-ENERGY-STAR-PRODUCT-DATA<br>STD-FUELECONOMY-VEHICLES<br>STD-WATERSENSE-FIXTURES | PARTIAL_BY_PRODUCT_FAMILY |
| exact-proposed-equipment-record | Exact Proposed Equipment Record Resolution | Proposed native rating or intensity terms. | Product-bearing ITC categories | Exact proposed manufacturer, model, or basic-model identity.<br>Product class, status, and effective date. | One unambiguous compatible proposed record and its source-native ratings. | Product-family-native | STD-DOE-CCMS-RATINGS<br>STD-ENERGY-STAR-PRODUCT-DATA<br>STD-FUELECONOMY-VEHICLES<br>STD-WATERSENSE-FIXTURES | PARTIAL_BY_PRODUCT_FAMILY |
| requirement-proposed-equipment-record | Requirements-Based Proposed Equipment Resolution | Proposed rating or intensity when an exact model is unavailable. | Product-bearing ITC categories | All mandatory project constraints.<br>Capacity, class, configuration, maximum consumption or flow, status, and effective date. | One unique qualifying record or a typed ambiguity result with candidates. | Product-family-native RECORD_SET and scalar fields | STD-ENERGY-STAR-PRODUCT-DATA<br>STD-DOE-CCMS-RATINGS<br>STD-WATERSENSE-FIXTURES<br>STD-FEMP-EXTERIOR-LIGHTING | IMPLEMENTED_FOR_RETAINED_ENERGY_STAR_DISHWASHERS; ACCESS_BLOCKED_OR_EXTENSION_ELSEWHERE |
| installed-equipment-context-baseline | Context-Matched Installed-Equipment Baseline | Existing intensity, power, or flow only when exact equipment is unavailable. | Equipment replacement ITC categories | Category and equipment class.<br>Capacity and operating context.<br>Geography or vintage when supported.<br>Pinned authoritative installed-stock population. | Selected scalar baseline with population, filters, sample size, selection method, and uncertainty. | Category-native, PER_UNIT, PER_EVENT, or PER_HOUR | STD-CONTEXT-BENCHMARKS | EXTEND_STANDARD_PER_CATEGORY |
| business-activity-resolution | Recognizable Business Activity Resolution | Annual events, hours, meals, racks, cycles, occupied area, throughput, or equivalent driver. | Activity-driven ITC categories | Recognizable business type.<br>Explicit scale driver.<br>Location and calendar.<br>Category-specific eligibility. | Annual activity driver with source population, method, and uncertainty. | events/year, hours/year, units/year, area, or throughput/year | STD-CONTEXT-BENCHMARKS<br>STD-OPERATING-SCHEDULE | EXTEND_CONTEXT_BENCHMARKS_PER_DRIVER |
| activity-to-annual-resource | Activity-to-Annual Resource Conversion | Annual baseline and proposed resource. | All event-, hour-, cycle-, and throughput-driven formulas | Source-backed or project-owned activity.<br>Compatible source-native intensity.<br>Equipment count.<br>Explicit idle and active split when applicable. | Annual resource by resource. | kWh/year, therm/year, MMBtu/year, gallons/year, or fuel-unit/year | Applicable product Standard<br>STD-CONTEXT-BENCHMARKS<br>STD-OPERATING-SCHEDULE | NEW_CATEGORY_LOCAL_PROCESS_ONLY |

## Possible new or extended Standards

### NEW STD-PROJECT-DOCUMENT-EVIDENCE - Project Document Evidence Contract

- Purpose: Turn caller-owned documents into reviewable typed evidence claims with exact provenance.
- Likely categories and processes: ITC-01 through ITC-54, project-document-evidence-extraction.
- Authoritative source: The immutable caller-provided document itself; no external catalog can replace it.
- Acquisition method: Hash bytes, detect MIME, parse or OCR in isolation, retain source span, and retain reviewer decision.
- Exact fields or equation: document_sha256; mime_type; page; span_or_bbox; field_key; raw_value; normalized_value; unit; extraction_method; confidence; review_status.
- Required inputs: Immutable document bytes.; Declared document type.; Category input schema..
- Exact output: Typed project-owned input claims..
- Unit and scope: Field-native, PROJECT_OWNED_EVIDENCE.
- Internal tables: project_documents, project_document_claims, project_document_reviews.
- Adapter design: Content-address documents, isolate format-specific extraction, require exact source locations, validate units, and fail missing or ambiguous fields closed for human review.
- Feasibility: HIGH for structured documents and MEDIUM for scanned documents.
- Evidence already available: Existing project-owned input contracts and provenance schema patterns.
- Remaining implementation work: Implement format adapters, OCR isolation, review workflow, schemas, mutation tests, and privacy retention policy.

### NEW STD-COMMERCIAL-DHW-LOAD - Commercial Domestic Hot-Water Load

- Purpose: Calculate delivered hot-water thermal load and input resource from explicit volume, activity, or profile and an explicit temperature boundary.
- Likely categories and processes: ITC-06, ITC-07, ITC-08, ITC-09, ITC-32, ITC-35, ITC-52, ITC-53, commercial-hot-water-load.
- Authoritative source: Project-owned metered or activity evidence plus pinned thermophysical constants. Any business-type default must be separately registered under STD-CONTEXT-BENCHMARKS.
- Acquisition method: Accept validated project inputs and retrieve only retained, versioned constants and separately approved context registrations.
- Exact fields or equation: Q_delivered = volume * density * cp * (T_setpoint - T_inlet); input_resource = Q_delivered / heater_efficiency.
- Required inputs: Volume profile or activity times hot-water per event.; Inlet temperature.; Setpoint temperature.; Fuel.; Heater efficiency.; Building and booster boundary..
- Exact output: Hot-water volume, delivered thermal energy, and purchased input resource..
- Unit and scope: gallons/year or interval, kWh-thermal/year, and input kWh, therm, or MMBtu/year.
- Internal tables: model_versions, model_input_schemas, calculation_assumptions, calculation_runs, calculation_source_dependencies.
- Adapter design: Typed thermodynamic core with explicit stages, unit-safe conversion, interval and annual profiles, and separate activity resolvers.
- Feasibility: HIGH for the exact-input branch.
- Evidence already available: The implemented commercial dishwasher boundary and unit-conversion proof provides a narrow reusable pattern.
- Remaining implementation work: Implement generic stage-aware input schemas, constants, exact tests, mutation cases, and vetted activity composition.

### NEW STD-PROJECT-RESOURCE-USAGE - Project Meter and Bill Resource Usage

- Purpose: Normalize caller-owned interval and bill usage into coverage-validated whole-meter evidence.
- Likely categories and processes: Potentially every ITC category, metered-resource-baseline.
- Authoritative source: Green Button ESPI, a customer utility export, or a utility bill.
- Acquisition method: Import caller-authorized files, hash bytes, retain meter and coverage metadata, and normalize without network calls during estimates.
- Exact fields or equation: meter_or_resource_identity; interval_start; interval_duration; value; unit_of_measure; billing_start; billing_end; billing_usage; time_zone; coverage_and_gap_flags; document_sha256.
- Required inputs: Authorized utility data or complete bills.; Meter and resource identity.; Time zone and coverage period..
- Exact output: Validated interval series and annual whole-meter totals with explicit gaps..
- Unit and scope: Resource-native interval and annual units, WHOLE_METER.
- Internal tables: project_resource_meters, project_resource_intervals, project_resource_bills, calculation_source_dependencies.
- Adapter design: Source-specific importers feeding one strict interval and billing schema with unit, time-zone, overlap, and gap validation.
- Feasibility: HIGH for Green Button ESPI and source-specific for CSV and PDF.
- Evidence already available: The interval tariff and provenance architecture supplies reusable interval and source-binding patterns.
- Remaining implementation work: Implement importers, coverage rules, fixtures, privacy controls, and mutation tests.

## Remaining automation plan

1. [IN_PROGRESS] model packaging: Close the remaining stale-receipt and path-safety findings in post-hoc model replay, then rerun the focused storage, ECR, replay, and offline rebuild suites.
2. [NOT_STARTED] source work: Regenerate the research database, proof ledger, category documents, and all derived documentation from the final source state.
3. [NOT_STARTED] tests: Run full real, portable, container, typecheck, application build, audit, and generated-view validation.
4. [NOT_STARTED] source work: Commit the frozen implementation source without the post-hoc replay receipt.
5. [NOT_STARTED] model packaging: Replay all four exact local images against the frozen commit and write the content-bound post-hoc replay receipt.
6. [NOT_STARTED] model packaging: Commit the post-hoc replay receipt as a separate evidence checkpoint.
7. [NOT_STARTED] tests: Run the complete content-bound real proof attestation, regenerate the ledger and documents, and commit the resulting run record and generated evidence.
8. [NOT_STARTED] AWS migration: Generate and commit the fresh 210-package storage manifest and migration report.
9. [NOT_STARTED] AWS migration: Create deterministic source archives as needed and commit their manifest identities.
10. [NOT_STARTED] AWS migration: Upload every required package to immutable research S3 keys and record exact object version IDs.
11. [NOT_STARTED] AWS migration: Verify every exact S3 version, restore every package, and byte-compare every restored SHA-256 and size before committing the checkpoint.
12. [NOT_STARTED] AWS migration: Run cleanup readiness and the complete offline suite, then commit the validated cleanup state.
13. [NOT_STARTED] AWS migration: Delete only exact verified temporary local packages and audit every retained local path.
14. [NOT_STARTED] AWS migration: Restore every package again from exact S3 versions and commit the restore proof.
15. [NOT_STARTED] model packaging: Restore all four exact ECR digests, replay verifiers, run full offline validation, and remove only the exact verified local image references.
16. [NOT_STARTED] AWS migration: Revalidate cleanup, perform the second exact cleanup pass, and produce the final local artifact audit and migration report.
17. [NOT_STARTED] documentation: Run final checks, push all commits, update draft PR #30, and monitor CI without merging, deploying, or modifying PR #29.

## Remaining effort

The current estimate is 4 to 8 additional automation hours if AWS, Docker, GitHub, and local disk remain available.
Lawful manual CCMS and WaterSense exports remain external human inputs and must stay fail-closed until provided.
